arXiv cs.AIresearch점수 72026-11-05맥락 확인 필요

Same Behavior, Different Mechanism: What Finite Experiments Can Certify About Integrated Information

Interpretability, AI auditing and assessments of machine consciousness increasingly rest on mechanism-level quantities: functionals of a system's causal transition law that describe how it computes, not what it outputs. Experiments see that law only through finite, partial and noisy interventions. We ask when such a quantity is identifiable from an experiment's access, continuous in the mechanism and certifiable from finite data, and answer exactly for a deliberately demanding case - the integrated information $\varphi$ of integrated information theory - in stochastic networks of four and five units with known ground truth. Behavior can be silent: a recurrent and a feed-forward network with identical input-output laws at their response delay carry 0.28 and 0 bits, yet internal recordings separate them within 4-53 trials. Partial access can be just as blind: in our examples, units left out of a one-step recording impose precision floors of 0.30-1.02 bits that no sample size lowers, and in all-but-self parity networks, recordings of any length see several hidden units only through the product of their fidelities - a symmetry that one intervention broke in exact checks. The measure has sharp edges: because $\varphi$ selects a partition, and the cause and effect it specifies, by one criterion and reports another, infinitesimal changes of mechanism that cross an edge change it by 25-100% at 99% of the edges we found in random networks, and about 1% of surrogate models that fit interventional data almost perfectly misreport it by more than 20%. One-sided claims survive: the same data certify a lower bound on both sides of an edge, and we prove $\varphi>1.004$ bits throughout a 64-dimensional region of mechanisms and certify it from about $10^7$ controlled transitions with false-certificate probability at most $α$. Measurability is thus a design question with computable answers.

SwarmEvolve: Latent Agent Swarms for Evolutionary Discovery and Optimization

LLM-guided evolutionary search uses AI agents to improve programs for mathematical and computational problems by iteratively refining solutions and combining high-scoring methods. However, the process is often slow because the LLM generates a long textual chain of thought for each round and must repeat this generation many times to maintain population diversity. We present SwarmEvolve, a framework in which hundreds of low-cost agents per round work in swarms, reason in latent space without decoding to text, and share information by passing KV caches. Each swarm studies the problem from a different angle. A novelty filter keeps the most distinct agent ideas by directly measuring the distances between the agents' hidden state vectors. Crossover of ideas happens naturally in latent space, as a summarizer agent receives the combined KV caches of all swarm members. On 21 problems in mathematical discovery and scientific computing, SwarmEvolve gets the highest average score and needs a median of only 3 to 8 minutes to reach the best scores that other state-of-the-art frameworks reach in 60 minutes. On several problems, SwarmEvolve with Qwen3 8B improves on the solutions that AlphaEvolve first found with frontier LLMs. Our work shows that agent swarms that communicate in latent space offer a fast, low-cost approach to program search for scientific problems.

arXiv cs.AIresearch점수 62026-11-05맥락 확인 필요

Towards interpreting any residual stream direction by scaling activation inversion

To interpret a direction in a language model's residual stream, we can look for text that strongly activates it. But when such text is found by scanning a corpus, it is limited to what that corpus contains. We develop Maximally-Activating Example Metamodels (MAEMs), language models which map residual stream directions from a subject model to text exemplars that strongly activate those directions. Our objective is to maximise the cosine similarity of a supplied residual stream direction with the activations of the text generated by the MAEM. This label-free RL objective helps to scale MAEMs on diverse sources of residual stream directions. Empirically, we find the resultant metamodel produces informative exemplars: a Qwen3.6-27B MAEM inverts held-out activations better than corpus search or a natural-language autoencoder (NLA), beats corpus search (at 10M tokens) on a majority of held-out languages/domains, and activates 89% of held-out SAE features. It also identifies concepts behind steering vectors about as well as NLAs, recovers the payloads of LoRA backdoors more often than corpus search, and can even verbalise some model workspace inner monologue. Unlike explanations produced by other metamodels (e.g. NLAs), MAEM exemplars can be verified with a forward pass on the subject model, making the MAEM objective a grounded alternative for scaling the training of metamodels.

ITWorld Koreakorean_editorial점수 82026-11-09맥락 확인 필요

메타, 기업용 AI 약관 ‘유턴’... 고객 반발에 시행 무기한 보류

메타가 기업 고객을 대상으로 한 새 서비스 약관의 시행을 보류했다. 약관은 고객 웹사이트의 모든 콘텐츠를 메타가 가져갈 수 있는 권리를 부여하는 내용이었다. 여기에 더해, 결함 있는 AI가 만들어내는 모든 오류의 책임을 고객에게 넘기는 조항도 담겨 있었다. 새 서비스 약관은 10월 19일 공개됐으며 당초 10월 30일 시행될 예정이었다. 메타는 이후 시행일을 11월 20일로 늦췄다. 그러나 짧은 연기만으로는 고객의 반발을 잠재우지 못했다. 메타는 결국 페이스북 비즈니스 도움말 센터에 업데이트를 무기한 보류한다는 공지 를 올렸다. 공지에 따르면 메타는 11월 5일 약관 미리보기 링크를 내렸다. 보류 대상은 메타 상업 약관, 제품 카탈로그 약관, 셀프서비스 광고 약관, 광고 크리에이티브 생성형 AI 약관 등 4종이다. 메타는 새 일정은 정하지 않았으며 기존 약관의 효력은 그대로 유지된다고 밝혔다. 반발의 강도를 감안하면 메타의 입장 선회는 놀라운 일이 아니다. 약관은 고객에게 지나치게 가혹한 조건으로 받아들여졌을 뿐 아니라, 영국 규제 당국이 최근 경고한 것과 같은 법적 지뢰밭 에 고객을 노출할 소지가 있었다. 하버드대 매체 니먼랩(Nieman Lab)의 보도 이후 법률 전문가 3명이 약관을 검토했다. 전문가는 약관 문구가 메타의 뉴스 사이트 콘텐츠 수집과 AI 학습 활용을 주장하는 근거로 쓰일 수 있다고 분석했다. 메타는 약관 개정이 뉴스 사이트 수집이 아니라 제3자 웹사이트의 판매 상품 정보를 정확히 확보하기 위한 것이며 웹 크롤러 운용 방식도 달라지지 않는다고 반박했다. 다만 보도 이후 약관에서 ‘학습’이라는 단어를 삭제했다. 규제와 정치권의 압박도 커졌다. 영국 경쟁시장청(CMA)은 10월 30일 메타가 시장 지배적 지위를 남용해 게시자와 광고주에게 부당한 조건을 강요했는지 정식 조사에 착수했다. 미국에서는 상원의원 애덤 시프(민주·캘리포니아)와 마샤 블랙번(공화·테네시)이 메타 CEO 마크 저커버그에게 서한을 보내 약관 철회를 요구했다. 메타는 니먼랩 에 보낸 성명에서 이해관계자와 계속 소통하며 충분한 시간을 들여 약관을 제대로 마련하겠다는 방침에 따라 변경을 보류한다고 밝혔다. 메타는 최근 잇따른 악재에 직면해 있다. 2주 전 고객 데이터를 불법적으로 사용한 혐의로 과징금을 부과받았고 , 5월에는 직원 8,000명을 감원했다 . dl-itworldkorea@foundryco.com

Google Workspace Updatesofficial점수 72026-10-29맥락 확인 필요

Create, preview, and edit files directly in the Gemini app

We’re introducing new ways to create and refine files and code in the Gemini app. You can now generate, preview, and edit files right within your Gemini app chat—expand to full screen to type edits directly, highlight text to ask questions, or have Gemini refine them. PDF preview and editing is supported on both the web and mobile, while all other formats (documents, spreadsheets, slide decks, Markdown files, TXT files, and code) are supported on the web only. Here are just some of the new things you can do: Generate and refine slide decks: Create presentation outlines or slide content, preview the layout in chat, and edit directly or ask Gemini to adjust the tone and structure before opening in Google Slides, or downloading as a PPTX or PDF. Build and export Google Sheets: Generate structured trackers, tables, and data models from a prompt, preview them in Gemini, and open them directly in Google Sheets. Edit documents and PDFs: Quickly draft PDFs, Google Docs, LaTeX, Markdown, or TXT files, and make manual or prompted edits inline on web or in a full-screen view—plus you can upload all of them to Drive. These capabilities are now available to all users in all languages where the Gemini app is available. Getting started Admins: There is no admin control for this feature. Visit the Help Center to learn more about turning the Gemini app on or off . End users: Users of all ages who have access to the Gemini app can generate files. To get started, ask Gemini to generate your desired file type. Visit the Help Center to learn more . Rollout pace Rapid Release and Scheduled Release domains: Available now Availability Available to all Google Workspace customers, Workspace Individual subscribers, and users with personal Google accounts Resources News from Google: You can now preview and edit documents, PDFs, presentations, spreadsheets, and more directly in Gemini Google Workspace Updates Blog: Move from conversation to creation with file generation in Gemini Gemini Apps Help: Use Gemini Apps

Google Workspace Updatesofficial점수 72026-11-05맥락 확인 필요

Now available in beta: Automatically apply client-side encryption (CSE) to confidential Docs, Sheets, Slides, and Drive files

Administrators can automatically apply client-side encryption (CSE) to control access to sensitive files across their organization using Policy-Based Enforcement, now available in beta. Previously, end users were required to manually create encrypted copies of existing files and remember to delete the unencrypted originals. With Policy-Based Enforcement, CSE integrates directly with the Google Workspace Rules Engine, enabling admins to set up customizable rules that seamlessly layer onto existing data governance strategies and applies CSE to the most sensitive, private data. Encryption is enforced “in place”, preserving the file’s original URL. This provides organizations with an authoritative, scalable control to protect critical data and satisfy rigorous compliance mandates, including ITAR, EAR, and HIPAA. End-user notification of admin led additional encryption. Key capabilities in beta: Comprehensive file and location support: Policy-Based Enforcement automatically encrypts files matching rule conditions across both my Drive and shared drives . Policy-Based Enforcement scans all file types supported by Workspace Rules , including Google Docs, Sheets, and Slides, Microsoft Office files (.docx, .xlsx, .pptx), and additional non-native formats such as PDFs and images. End-user transparency: When users open a file encrypted by an administrative policy, they receive an in-product contextual notification informing them of the update. Audit logging and proactive alerting: Admins can monitor encryption activity and outcomes within the security investigation tool (SIT) and will receive alert center notifications if their external Key Access Control List Service (KACLS) is unreachable or experiencing issues. Getting started Admins: Enrollment required: This feature is available in beta to eligible customers. To request access, complete the Policy-Based Enforcement beta Application with your domain information. Customers must have Assured Controls or Assured Controls Plus licenses to enable the feature. Configuration: This feature is OFF by default and can be configured in the Admin console. Once allowlisted, eligible domains will receive onboarding and configuration documentation. End users: There is no end-user setting for this feature. When opening a file that has been encrypted by a rule, users will see an informational banner indicating that additional encryption was applied by their administrator. Rollout pace Rapid Release and Scheduled Release domains: Available now for eligible customers enrolled in the Policy-Based Enforcement beta allowlist Availability Available to Google Workspace customers with the Google Workspace Assured Controls or Assured Controls Plus add-on to Enterprise Plus who have enrolled in the Policy-Based Enforcement beta . Resources Google Workspace Admin Help: About client-side encryption Google Workspace Admin Help: Apply client-side encryption using data protection rules (beta) Google Docs Editors Help: Get started with encrypted files in Drive, Docs, Sheets & Slides Beta Program Registration: Policy-Based Enforcement beta application
